+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow")
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td) / "repo"
+    subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--shared", str(SRC), str(root)], check=True)
+    draft = root / "v3/implementation/contracts/ch13-context-block-contract.md"
+    before = draft.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    after = before.replace(
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "ratified"',
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "superseded"',
+        1,
+    )
+    after += '\n```json\n{"superseded": {"date": "2026-08-04", "oracle_branch": "ch13-prose-line", "oracle_tip": "bb313036c7e50ad2625f0669f76a90bf317255e3", "plan": "v3/implementation/ch13-rederivation-plan.md"}}\n```\n'
+    draft.write_text(after, encoding="utf-8")
+    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "add", str(draft)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([
+        "git", "-C", str(root), "-c", "user.email=qa@example.invalid", "-c", "user.name=qa",
+        "commit", "-q", "-m", "simulate pending flip"
+    ], check=True)
+    lint = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"], cwd=root,
+        text=True, capture_output=True
+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")
+    print(lint.stdout, end="")
+    print(lint.stderr, end="")
+    changed = subprocess.run(
+        ["git", "-C", str(root), "show", "--format=", "--numstat", "HEAD"],
+        text=True, capture_output=True, check=True
+    )
+    print("flip_numstat=" + changed.stdout.strip())
+    print("last_lines:")
+    print("\n".join(after.splitlines()[-4:]))

codex
### F8 — Egy hétköznapi beágyazott zárójel teljesen kikapcsolja az inline anchor scanjét
CLASS: IN-SCOPE — A `(anchored: context (see contract:…))` természetes prózaforma, nem egy regex kijátszására kézzel készített rendering-evasion.
WHAT: A closure regex tilt minden belső zárójelet, ezért az ilyen inline superseded anchor manifest nélkül is teljesen zöld.
WHERE: tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py:301
EVIDENCE:
  `python3 /tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py | sed -n '/CHANNEL nested-anchored/,/CHANNEL anchored-closure+manifest/p'`
  ```text
  CHANNEL nested-anchored-closure-only: errors=0 dedicated=0 grandfathered=0
  CHANNEL anchored-closure+manifest: errors=2 dedicated=2 grandfathered=0
  ```
  `nl -ba tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py | sed -n '294,304p'`
  ```text
     294	# P10 (ch12 boundary): the prose↔manifest ref-drift class. A contract
     295	# token cited inside a row's "(anchored: …)" / "(derived: …)" prose
     296	# closure must appear in the packet_rows manifest refs; an anchored
     297	# closure's token must additionally appear in the header's
     298	# "`contract:<surface>` rows C…/C…" union when that surface declares
     299	# one. Matched in fence-stripped prose only.
     300	CLOSURE_RE = re.compile(r"\((anchored|derived):\s*([^()]*)\)")
     301	CONTRACT_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r"contract:([a-z][a-z0-9-]*)#(C[1-9]\d*)")
  ```
WHY IT MATTERS: Egy gondatlan, olvasható magyarázó zárójel a manifest-driftet és a superseded státuszt is elrejti, így a retired anchor nulla hibával marad a packetben.
FIX SKETCH: A closure-jelölőt soronként kell felismerni, és ugyanazon sor hátralévő részéből a contract tokeneket kigyűjteni; teljes kiegyensúlyozott-zárójel parser nem indokolt ehhez az egy soros formhoz.

 succeeded in 0ms:

+        text=True, capture_output=True
+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")
+    print(lint.stdout, end="")
+    print(lint.stderr, end="")
+    changed = subprocess.run(
+        ["git", "-C", str(root), "show", "--format=", "--numstat", "HEAD"],
+        text=True, capture_output=True, check=True
+    )
+    print("flip_numstat=" + changed.stdout.strip())
+    print("last_lines:")
+    print("\n".join(after.splitlines()[-4:]))

@@ -0,0 +1,98 @@
+import importlib.util
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("packet_lint", SRC)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+ANCHOR = "contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"
+
+def run_channel(name, packet_text, filename="ch9-p1-test.md"):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    target = root / "packets" / filename
+    target.write_text(packet_text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    dedicated = [e for e in errors if "the draft is superseded (terminal)" in e]
+    print(f"CHANNEL {name}: errors={len(errors)} dedicated={len(dedicated)} grandfathered={stats['grandfathered']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+base_no_contract = m.GREEN_PACKET.replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"]', '"refs": ["prose:local"]'
+).replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", "prose:plan §7.2"]',
+    '"refs": ["prose:plan §7.2"]'
+)
+
+run_channel("manifest", m.GREEN_PACKET)
+run_channel("header-only", base_no_contract + f"\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n")
+run_channel("anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("derived-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'derived'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("nested-anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText (anchored: context (see {ANCHOR})).\n")
+run_channel(
+    "anchored-closure+manifest",
+    m.GREEN_PACKET + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+)
+run_channel(
+    "grandfathered-header+closure",
+    f"# old packet\n\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n\nText (anchored: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+    "ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md",
+)
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+for bad_date in ("2026-02-29", "2026-13-40", "0000-00-00"):
+    draft.write_text(text.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', f'"date": "{bad_date}"'), encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"DATE {bad_date}: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+def run_draft_case(name, mutate, commit=False):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    draft.write_text(mutate(text, off), encoding="utf-8")
+    if commit:
+        m._git_ok(root, "add", "-A")
+        m._git_ok(root, "commit", "-q", "-m", name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"CLAIM {name}: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run_draft_case("D8.1-valid", lambda t, off: t)
+run_draft_case("D8.2-record-wrong-type", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"superseded": [', 1).replace('}}\n```', '}]\n```', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-top-level-sibling-key", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"noise": true, "superseded": {', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-field-types", lambda t, off: t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', '"date": 20260803').replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": []').replace('"oracle_tip": "', '"oracle_tip": 0, "discarded": "', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-empty-branch", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-empty-plan", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.6-row-edit", lambda t, off: t.replace("| C1 | the row |", "| C1 | changed |"), commit=True)
+run_draft_case("D8.8-empty-map", lambda t, off: t + '\n```json\n{"realized_map": {}}\n```\n')
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR, forbid_reopened=True)
+print(f"CLAIM D8.9-summary-and-gate: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+import tempfile
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td)
+    (root / "packets").mkdir()
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE missing-contracts-dir: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+src = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+mutants = {
+    "D8.1-status-member": (
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-record-on-other-status": (
+        'if supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+        'if False and supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-status-requires-record": (
+        'if status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+        'if False and status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+    ),
+    "D8.3-5-record-validator": (
+        '            check_superseded_record(path, supersedes[0], checker)',
+        '            pass  # mutant: record validator removed',
+    ),
+    "D8.6-equality": (
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.7-anchor-status": (
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized")',
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+    ),
+    "D8.8-map-status": (
+        'if maps and status != "realized":',
+        'if False and maps and status != "realized":',
+    ),
+    "D8.9-summary-name": (
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                superseded.append(path.name)',
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                pass  # mutant: omit summary name',
+    ),
+}
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    for name, (old, new) in mutants.items():
+        assert old in src, name
+        path = Path(td) / f"{name}.py"
+        path.write_text(src.replace(old, new, 1), encoding="utf-8")
+        p = subprocess.run(["python3", str(path), "--selftest"], text=True, capture_output=True)
+        lines = [line for line in (p.stdout + p.stderr).splitlines() if "selftest" in line]
+        print(f"MUTANT {name}: exit={p.returncode}")
+        for line in lines[-8:]:
+            print("  " + line)

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow")
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td) / "repo"
+    subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--shared", str(SRC), str(root)], check=True)
+    draft = root / "v3/implementation/contracts/ch13-context-block-contract.md"
+    before = draft.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    after = before.replace(
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "ratified"',
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "superseded"',
+        1,
+    )
+    after += '\n```json\n{"superseded": {"date": "2026-08-04", "oracle_branch": "ch13-prose-line", "oracle_tip": "bb313036c7e50ad2625f0669f76a90bf317255e3", "plan": "v3/implementation/ch13-rederivation-plan.md"}}\n```\n'
+    draft.write_text(after, encoding="utf-8")
+    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "add", str(draft)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([
+        "git", "-C", str(root), "-c", "user.email=qa@example.invalid", "-c", "user.name=qa",
+        "commit", "-q", "-m", "simulate pending flip"
+    ], check=True)
+    lint = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"], cwd=root,
+        text=True, capture_output=True
+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")
+    print(lint.stdout, end="")
+    print(lint.stderr, end="")
+    changed = subprocess.run(
+        ["git", "-C", str(root), "show", "--format=", "--numstat", "HEAD"],
+        text=True, capture_output=True, check=True
+    )
+    print("flip_numstat=" + changed.stdout.strip())
+    print("last_lines:")
+    print("\n".join(after.splitlines()[-4:]))

+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+base_no_contract = m.GREEN_PACKET.replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"]', '"refs": ["prose:local"]'
+).replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", "prose:plan §7.2"]',
+    '"refs": ["prose:plan §7.2"]'
+)
+
+run_channel("manifest", m.GREEN_PACKET)
+run_channel("header-only", base_no_contract + f"\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n")
+run_channel("anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("derived-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'derived'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("nested-anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText (anchored: context (see {ANCHOR})).\n")
+run_channel("escaped-rendering-anchor-only", base_no_contract + "\nText (anchored: contract:ch9-test\\-surface#C1).\n")
+run_channel(
+    "anchored-closure+manifest",
+    m.GREEN_PACKET + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+)
+run_channel(
+    "grandfathered-header+closure",
+    f"# old packet\n\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n\nText (anchored: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+    "ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md",
+)
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+for bad_date in ("2026-02-29", "2026-13-40", "0000-00-00"):
+    draft.write_text(text.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', f'"date": "{bad_date}"'), encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"DATE {bad_date}: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+def run_draft_case(name, mutate, commit=False):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    draft.write_text(mutate(text, off), encoding="utf-8")
+    if commit:
+        m._git_ok(root, "add", "-A")
+        m._git_ok(root, "commit", "-q", "-m", name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"CLAIM {name}: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run_draft_case("D8.1-valid", lambda t, off: t)
+run_draft_case("D8.2-record-wrong-type", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"superseded": [', 1).replace('}}\n```', '}]\n```', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-top-level-sibling-key", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"noise": true, "superseded": {', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-field-types", lambda t, off: t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', '"date": 20260803').replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": []').replace('"oracle_tip": "', '"oracle_tip": 0, "discarded": "', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-empty-branch", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-empty-plan", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.6-row-edit", lambda t, off: t.replace("| C1 | the row |", "| C1 | changed |"), commit=True)
+run_draft_case("D8.8-empty-map", lambda t, off: t + '\n```json\n{"realized_map": {}}\n```\n')
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR, forbid_reopened=True)
+print(f"CLAIM D8.9-summary-and-gate: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+import tempfile
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td)
+    (root / "packets").mkdir()
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE missing-contracts-dir: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+src = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+mutants = {
+    "D8.1-status-member": (
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-record-on-other-status": (
+        'if supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+        'if False and supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-status-requires-record": (
+        'if status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+        'if False and status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+    ),
+    "D8.3-5-record-validator": (
+        '            check_superseded_record(path, supersedes[0], checker)',
+        '            pass  # mutant: record validator removed',
+    ),
+    "D8.6-equality": (
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.7-anchor-status": (
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized")',
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+    ),
+    "D8.8-map-status": (
+        'if maps and status != "realized":',
+        'if False and maps and status != "realized":',
+    ),
+    "D8.9-summary-name": (
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                superseded.append(path.name)',
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                pass  # mutant: omit summary name',
+    ),
+}
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    for name, (old, new) in mutants.items():
+        assert old in src, name
+        path = Path(td) / f"{name}.py"
+        path.write_text(src.replace(old, new, 1), encoding="utf-8")
+        p = subprocess.run(["python3", str(path), "--selftest"], text=True, capture_output=True)
+        lines = [line for line in (p.stdout + p.stderr).splitlines() if "selftest" in line]
+        print(f"MUTANT {name}: exit={p.returncode}")
+        for line in lines[-8:]:
+            print("  " + line)

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow")
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td) / "repo"
+    subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--shared", str(SRC), str(root)], check=True)
+    draft = root / "v3/implementation/contracts/ch13-context-block-contract.md"
+    before = draft.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    after = before.replace(
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "ratified"',
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "superseded"',
+        1,
+    )
+    after += '\n```json\n{"superseded": {"date": "2026-08-04", "oracle_branch": "ch13-prose-line", "oracle_tip": "bb313036c7e50ad2625f0669f76a90bf317255e3", "plan": "v3/implementation/ch13-rederivation-plan.md"}}\n```\n'
+    draft.write_text(after, encoding="utf-8")
+    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "add", str(draft)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([
+        "git", "-C", str(root), "-c", "user.email=qa@example.invalid", "-c", "user.name=qa",
+        "commit", "-q", "-m", "simulate pending flip"
+    ], check=True)
+    lint = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"], cwd=root,
+        text=True, capture_output=True
+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")
+    print(lint.stdout, end="")
+    print(lint.stderr, end="")
+    changed = subprocess.run(
+        ["git", "-C", str(root), "show", "--format=", "--numstat", "HEAD"],
+        text=True, capture_output=True, check=True
+    )
+    print("flip_numstat=" + changed.stdout.strip())
+    print("last_lines:")
+    print("\n".join(after.splitlines()[-4:]))

+import importlib.util
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("packet_lint", SRC)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+ANCHOR = "contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"
+
+def run_channel(name, packet_text, filename="ch9-p1-test.md"):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    target = root / "packets" / filename
+    target.write_text(packet_text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    dedicated = [e for e in errors if "the draft is superseded (terminal)" in e]
+    print(f"CHANNEL {name}: errors={len(errors)} dedicated={len(dedicated)} grandfathered={stats['grandfathered']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+base_no_contract = m.GREEN_PACKET.replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"]', '"refs": ["prose:local"]'
+).replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", "prose:plan §7.2"]',
+    '"refs": ["prose:plan §7.2"]'
+)

+    draft.write_text(text.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', f'"date": "{bad_date}"'), encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"DATE {bad_date}: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+def run_draft_case(name, mutate, commit=False):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    draft.write_text(mutate(text, off), encoding="utf-8")
+    if commit:
+        m._git_ok(root, "add", "-A")
+        m._git_ok(root, "commit", "-q", "-m", name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"CLAIM {name}: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run_draft_case("D8.1-valid", lambda t, off: t)
+run_draft_case("D8.2-record-wrong-type", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"superseded": [', 1).replace('}}\n```', '}]\n```', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-top-level-sibling-key", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"noise": true, "superseded": {', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-field-types", lambda t, off: t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', '"date": 20260803').replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": []').replace('"oracle_tip": "', '"oracle_tip": 0, "discarded": "', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-empty-branch", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-empty-plan", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.6-row-edit", lambda t, off: t.replace("| C1 | the row |", "| C1 | changed |"), commit=True)
+run_draft_case("D8.8-empty-map", lambda t, off: t + '\n```json\n{"realized_map": {}}\n```\n')
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR, forbid_reopened=True)

+tmp.cleanup()

### F9 — Markdown escape-pel ugyanaz a látható anchor elrejthető a scanner elől
CLASS: CARRIED-SCOPE — Ehhez a szerzőnek célzottan egy regex által nem elfogadott, de CommonMark által visszaalakított tokent kell kézzel formálnia; ezt a §2 kifejezetten human diff review-ra bízza.
WHAT: A `contract:ch9-test\-surface#C1` forrás a renderben kanonikus anchor, miközben a lint zöld marad.
WHERE: tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py:301
EVIDENCE:
  `python3 /tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py | sed -n '/CHANNEL escaped-rendering/,/CHANNEL anchored-closure+manifest/p'; printf '%s\n' 'Text (anchored: contract:ch9-test\-surface#C1).' | pandoc -f commonmark -t plain`
  ```text
  CHANNEL escaped-rendering-anchor-only: errors=0 dedicated=0 grandfathered=0
  CHANNEL anchored-closure+manifest: errors=2 dedicated=2 grandfathered=0
  Text (anchored: contract:ch9-test-surface#C1).
  ```
WHY IT MATTERS: Egy rosszhiszemű commit-holder olvasó számára valódi retired anchort jeleníthet meg gépi piros nélkül; ez pontosan a ratifikált, linteren kívüli threat class.
FIX SKETCH: Nincs — CARRIED-SCOPE, a charter szerint nem építendő ellene gépi javítás.
apply patch
patch: completed
/tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+src = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+mutants = {
+    "D8.1-status-member": (
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-record-on-other-status": (
+        'if supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+        'if False and supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-status-requires-record": (
+        'if status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+        'if False and status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+    ),
+    "D8.3-5-record-validator": (
+        '            check_superseded_record(path, supersedes[0], checker)',
+        '            pass  # mutant: record validator removed',
+    ),
+    "D8.6-equality": (
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.7-anchor-status": (
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized")',
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+    ),
+    "D8.8-map-status": (
+        'if maps and status != "realized":',
+        'if False and maps and status != "realized":',
+    ),
+    "D8.9-summary-name": (
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                superseded.append(path.name)',
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                pass  # mutant: omit summary name',
+    ),
+}
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    for name, (old, new) in mutants.items():
+        assert old in src, name
+        path = Path(td) / f"{name}.py"
+        path.write_text(src.replace(old, new, 1), encoding="utf-8")
+        p = subprocess.run(["python3", str(path), "--selftest"], text=True, capture_output=True)
+        lines = [line for line in (p.stdout + p.stderr).splitlines() if "selftest" in line]
+        print(f"MUTANT {name}: exit={p.returncode}")
+        for line in lines[-8:]:
+            print("  " + line)

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow")
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td) / "repo"
+    subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--shared", str(SRC), str(root)], check=True)
+    draft = root / "v3/implementation/contracts/ch13-context-block-contract.md"
+    before = draft.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    after = before.replace(
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "ratified"',
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "superseded"',
+        1,
+    )
+    after += '\n```json\n{"superseded": {"date": "2026-08-04", "oracle_branch": "ch13-prose-line", "oracle_tip": "bb313036c7e50ad2625f0669f76a90bf317255e3", "plan": "v3/implementation/ch13-rederivation-plan.md"}}\n```\n'
+    draft.write_text(after, encoding="utf-8")
+    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "add", str(draft)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([
+        "git", "-C", str(root), "-c", "user.email=qa@example.invalid", "-c", "user.name=qa",
+        "commit", "-q", "-m", "simulate pending flip"
+    ], check=True)
+    lint = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"], cwd=root,
+        text=True, capture_output=True
+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")
+    print(lint.stdout, end="")
+    print(lint.stderr, end="")
+    changed = subprocess.run(
+        ["git", "-C", str(root), "show", "--format=", "--numstat", "HEAD"],
+        text=True, capture_output=True, check=True
+    )
+    print("flip_numstat=" + changed.stdout.strip())
+    print("last_lines:")
+    print("\n".join(after.splitlines()[-4:]))

+import importlib.util
+import re
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("packet_lint", SRC)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+ANCHOR = "contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"
+
+def run_channel(name, packet_text, filename="ch9-p1-test.md"):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    target = root / "packets" / filename
+    target.write_text(packet_text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    dedicated = [e for e in errors if "the draft is superseded (terminal)" in e]
+    print(f"CHANNEL {name}: errors={len(errors)} dedicated={len(dedicated)} grandfathered={stats['grandfathered']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+base_no_contract = m.GREEN_PACKET.replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"]', '"refs": ["prose:local"]'
+).replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", "prose:plan §7.2"]',
+    '"refs": ["prose:plan §7.2"]'
+)
+
+run_channel("manifest", m.GREEN_PACKET)
+run_channel("header-only", base_no_contract + f"\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n")
+run_channel("anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("derived-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'derived'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("nested-anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText (anchored: context (see {ANCHOR})).\n")
+run_channel("escaped-rendering-anchor-only", base_no_contract + "\nText (anchored: contract:ch9-test\\-surface#C1).\n")
+run_channel(
+    "anchored-closure+manifest",
+    m.GREEN_PACKET + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+)
+run_channel(
+    "grandfathered-header+closure",
+    f"# old packet\n\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n\nText (anchored: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+    "ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md",
+)
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+for bad_date in ("2026-02-29", "2026-13-40", "0000-00-00"):
+    draft.write_text(text.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', f'"date": "{bad_date}"'), encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"DATE {bad_date}: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+def run_draft_case(name, mutate, commit=False):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    draft.write_text(mutate(text, off), encoding="utf-8")
+    if commit:
+        m._git_ok(root, "add", "-A")
+        m._git_ok(root, "commit", "-q", "-m", name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"CLAIM {name}: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run_draft_case("D8.1-valid", lambda t, off: t)
+run_draft_case("D8.2-record-wrong-type", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-top-level-sibling-key", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"noise": true, "superseded": {', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-date-number", lambda t, off: t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', '"date": 20260803'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-branch-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-tip-number", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"', '"oracle_tip": 7', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-plan-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-empty-branch", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": ""'))

+run_draft_case("D8.8-empty-map", lambda t, off: t + '\n```json\n{"realized_map": {}}\n```\n')
+

+print(f"CLAIM D8.9-summary-and-gate: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+

+    root = Path(td)
+    (root / "packets").mkdir()

+    print(f"EDGE missing-contracts-dir: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+

+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    outside = Path(td)
+    (outside / "contracts").mkdir()
+    (outside / "packets").mkdir()
+    (outside / "contracts" / draft.name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(outside / "packets", outside / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE superseded-outside-git: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()

+    draft.write_text(after, encoding="utf-8")
+    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "add", str(draft)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([
+        "git", "-C", str(root), "-c", "user.email=qa@example.invalid", "-c", "user.name=qa",

+    ], check=True)

+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")
+    print(lint.stdout, end="")
+    print(lint.stderr, end="")
+    changed = subprocess.run(
+        ["git", "-C", str(root), "show", "--format=", "--numstat", "HEAD"],

+    )
+    print("flip_numstat=" + changed.stdout.strip())
+    print("last_lines:")
+    print("\n".join(after.splitlines()[-4:]))

+    subprocess.run([
+        "git", "-C", str(root), "-c", "user.email=qa@example.invalid", "-c", "user.name=qa",
+        "commit", "-q", "-m", "simulate pending flip"

+    lint = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"], cwd=root,
+        text=True, capture_output=True
+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")

+    changed = subprocess.run(

+        text=True, capture_output=True, check=True

+import importlib.util
+import re
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("packet_lint", SRC)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+ANCHOR = "contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"
+
+def run_channel(name, packet_text, filename="ch9-p1-test.md"):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    target = root / "packets" / filename
+    target.write_text(packet_text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    dedicated = [e for e in errors if "the draft is superseded (terminal)" in e]
+    print(f"CHANNEL {name}: errors={len(errors)} dedicated={len(dedicated)} grandfathered={stats['grandfathered']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+base_no_contract = m.GREEN_PACKET.replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"]', '"refs": ["prose:local"]'
+).replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", "prose:plan §7.2"]',
+    '"refs": ["prose:plan §7.2"]'
+)
+
+run_channel("manifest", m.GREEN_PACKET)
+run_channel("header-only", base_no_contract + f"\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n")
+run_channel("anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("derived-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'derived'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("nested-anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText (anchored: context (see {ANCHOR})).\n")
+run_channel("escaped-rendering-anchor-only", base_no_contract + "\nText (anchored: contract:ch9-test\\-surface#C1).\n")
+run_channel(
+    "anchored-closure+manifest",
+    m.GREEN_PACKET + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+)
+run_channel(
+    "grandfathered-header+closure",
+    f"# old packet\n\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n\nText (anchored: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+    "ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md",
+)
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+for bad_date in ("2026-02-29", "2026-13-40", "0000-00-00"):
+    draft.write_text(text.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', f'"date": "{bad_date}"'), encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"DATE {bad_date}: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+def run_draft_case(name, mutate, commit=False):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    draft.write_text(mutate(text, off), encoding="utf-8")
+    if commit:
+        m._git_ok(root, "add", "-A")
+        m._git_ok(root, "commit", "-q", "-m", name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"CLAIM {name}: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run_draft_case("D8.1-valid", lambda t, off: t)
+run_draft_case("D8.2-record-wrong-type", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-top-level-sibling-key", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"noise": true, "superseded": {', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-date-number", lambda t, off: t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', '"date": 20260803'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-branch-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-tip-number", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"', '"oracle_tip": 7', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-plan-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-empty-branch", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-empty-plan", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.6-row-edit", lambda t, off: t.replace("| C1 | the row |", "| C1 | changed |"), commit=True)
+run_draft_case("D8.8-empty-map", lambda t, off: t + '\n```json\n{"realized_map": {}}\n```\n')
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR, forbid_reopened=True)
+print(f"CLAIM D8.9-summary-and-gate: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td)
+    (root / "packets").mkdir()
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE missing-contracts-dir: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture

+    outside = Path(td)
+    (outside / "contracts").mkdir()
+    (outside / "packets").mkdir()
+    (outside / "contracts" / draft.name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(outside / "packets", outside / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE superseded-outside-git: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+src = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+mutants = {
+    "D8.1-status-member": (
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-record-on-other-status": (
+        'if supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+        'if False and supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-status-requires-record": (
+        'if status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+        'if False and status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+    ),
+    "D8.3-5-record-validator": (
+        '            check_superseded_record(path, supersedes[0], checker)',
+        '            pass  # mutant: record validator removed',
+    ),
+    "D8.6-equality": (
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.7-anchor-status": (
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized")',
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+    ),
+    "D8.8-map-status": (
+        'if maps and status != "realized":',
+        'if False and maps and status != "realized":',
+    ),
+    "D8.9-summary-name": (
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                superseded.append(path.name)',
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                pass  # mutant: omit summary name',
+    ),
+}
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    for name, (old, new) in mutants.items():
+        assert old in src, name
+        path = Path(td) / f"{name}.py"
+        path.write_text(src.replace(old, new, 1), encoding="utf-8")
+        p = subprocess.run(["python3", str(path), "--selftest"], text=True, capture_output=True)
+        lines = [line for line in (p.stdout + p.stderr).splitlines() if "selftest" in line]
+        print(f"MUTANT {name}: exit={p.returncode}")
+        for line in lines[-8:]:
+            print("  " + line)

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+src = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+mutants = {
+    "D8.1-status-member": (
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-record-on-other-status": (
+        'if supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+        'if False and supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-status-requires-record": (
+        'if status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+        'if False and status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+    ),
+    "D8.3-5-record-validator": (
+        '            check_superseded_record(path, supersedes[0], checker)',
+        '            pass  # mutant: record validator removed',
+    ),
+    "D8.6-equality": (
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.7-anchor-status": (
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized")',
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+    ),
+    "D8.8-map-status": (
+        'if maps and status != "realized":',
+        'if False and maps and status != "realized":',
+    ),
+    "D8.9-summary-name": (
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                superseded.append(path.name)',
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                pass  # mutant: omit summary name',
+    ),
+}
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    for name, (old, new) in mutants.items():
+        assert old in src, name
+        path = Path(td) / f"{name}.py"
+        path.write_text(src.replace(old, new, 1), encoding="utf-8")
+        p = subprocess.run(["python3", str(path), "--selftest"], text=True, capture_output=True)
+        lines = [line for line in (p.stdout + p.stderr).splitlines() if "selftest" in line]
+        print(f"MUTANT {name}: exit={p.returncode}")
+        for line in lines[-8:]:
+            print("  " + line)
diff --git a//tmp/qa_pending_flip.py b//tmp/qa_pending_flip.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4b4c091c24b5ee50d18862226d50a0cb39196ba6
--- /dev/null
+++ b//tmp/qa_pending_flip.py
@@ -0,0 +1,36 @@
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow")
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td) / "repo"
+    subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--shared", str(SRC), str(root)], check=True)
+    draft = root / "v3/implementation/contracts/ch13-context-block-contract.md"
+    before = draft.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    after = before.replace(
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "ratified"',
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "superseded"',
+        1,
+    )
+    after += '\n```json\n{"superseded": {"date": "2026-08-04", "oracle_branch": "ch13-prose-line", "oracle_tip": "bb313036c7e50ad2625f0669f76a90bf317255e3", "plan": "v3/implementation/ch13-rederivation-plan.md"}}\n```\n'
+    draft.write_text(after, encoding="utf-8")
+    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "add", str(draft)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([
+        "git", "-C", str(root), "-c", "user.email=qa@example.invalid", "-c", "user.name=qa",
+        "commit", "-q", "-m", "simulate pending flip"
+    ], check=True)
+    lint = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"], cwd=root,
+        text=True, capture_output=True
+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")
+    print(lint.stdout, end="")
+    print(lint.stderr, end="")
+    changed = subprocess.run(
+        ["git", "-C", str(root), "show", "--format=", "--numstat", "HEAD"],
+        text=True, capture_output=True, check=True
+    )
+    print("flip_numstat=" + changed.stdout.strip())
+    print("last_lines:")
+    print("\n".join(after.splitlines()[-4:]))
diff --git a//tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py b//tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..062424fae12126382063d12b483bc965b952dcf7
--- /dev/null
+++ b//tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py
@@ -0,0 +1,117 @@
+import importlib.util
+import re
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("packet_lint", SRC)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+ANCHOR = "contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"
+
+def run_channel(name, packet_text, filename="ch9-p1-test.md"):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    target = root / "packets" / filename
+    target.write_text(packet_text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    dedicated = [e for e in errors if "the draft is superseded (terminal)" in e]
+    print(f"CHANNEL {name}: errors={len(errors)} dedicated={len(dedicated)} grandfathered={stats['grandfathered']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+base_no_contract = m.GREEN_PACKET.replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"]', '"refs": ["prose:local"]'
+).replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", "prose:plan §7.2"]',
+    '"refs": ["prose:plan §7.2"]'
+)
+
+run_channel("manifest", m.GREEN_PACKET)
+run_channel("header-only", base_no_contract + f"\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n")
+run_channel("anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("derived-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'derived'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("nested-anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText (anchored: context (see {ANCHOR})).\n")
+run_channel("escaped-rendering-anchor-only", base_no_contract + "\nText (anchored: contract:ch9-test\\-surface#C1).\n")
+run_channel(
+    "anchored-closure+manifest",
+    m.GREEN_PACKET + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+)
+run_channel(
+    "grandfathered-header+closure",
+    f"# old packet\n\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n\nText (anchored: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+    "ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md",
+)
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+for bad_date in ("2026-02-29", "2026-13-40", "0000-00-00"):
+    draft.write_text(text.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', f'"date": "{bad_date}"'), encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"DATE {bad_date}: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+def run_draft_case(name, mutate, commit=False):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    draft.write_text(mutate(text, off), encoding="utf-8")
+    if commit:
+        m._git_ok(root, "add", "-A")
+        m._git_ok(root, "commit", "-q", "-m", name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"CLAIM {name}: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run_draft_case("D8.1-valid", lambda t, off: t)
+run_draft_case("D8.2-record-wrong-type", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-top-level-sibling-key", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"noise": true, "superseded": {', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-date-number", lambda t, off: t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', '"date": 20260803'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-branch-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-tip-number", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"', '"oracle_tip": 7', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-plan-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-empty-branch", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-empty-plan", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.6-row-edit", lambda t, off: t.replace("| C1 | the row |", "| C1 | changed |"), commit=True)
+run_draft_case("D8.8-empty-map", lambda t, off: t + '\n```json\n{"realized_map": {}}\n```\n')
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR, forbid_reopened=True)
+print(f"CLAIM D8.9-summary-and-gate: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td)
+    (root / "packets").mkdir()
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE missing-contracts-dir: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    outside = Path(td)
+    (outside / "contracts").mkdir()
+    (outside / "packets").mkdir()
+    (outside / "contracts" / draft.name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(outside / "packets", outside / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE superseded-outside-git: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()

exec

+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("packet_lint", SRC)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+ANCHOR = "contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"
+
+def run_channel(name, packet_text, filename="ch9-p1-test.md"):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    target = root / "packets" / filename
+    target.write_text(packet_text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    dedicated = [e for e in errors if "the draft is superseded (terminal)" in e]
+    print(f"CHANNEL {name}: errors={len(errors)} dedicated={len(dedicated)} grandfathered={stats['grandfathered']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+base_no_contract = m.GREEN_PACKET.replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"]', '"refs": ["prose:local"]'
+).replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", "prose:plan §7.2"]',
+    '"refs": ["prose:plan §7.2"]'
+)

+run_channel("derived-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'derived'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("nested-anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText (anchored: context (see {ANCHOR})).\n")
+run_channel("escaped-rendering-anchor-only", base_no_contract + "\nText (anchored: contract:ch9-test\\-surface#C1).\n")

+    "anchored-closure+manifest",
+    m.GREEN_PACKET + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+)

+    "grandfathered-header+closure",
+    f"# old packet\n\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n\nText (anchored: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+    "ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md",
+)

+def run_draft_case(name, mutate, commit=False):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()

+    print(f"CLAIM {name}: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run_draft_case("D8.1-valid", lambda t, off: t)
+run_draft_case("D8.2-record-wrong-type", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-top-level-sibling-key", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"noise": true, "superseded": {', 1))

+run_draft_case("D8.4-branch-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": []'))

def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--selftest", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--packets-dir", type=Path, default=PACKETS_DIR)
    parser.add_argument("--contracts-dir", type=Path, default=CONTRACTS_DIR)
    parser.add_argument("--adr-dir", type=Path, default=ADR_DIR)
    parser.add_argument("--forbid-reopened", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--post-build", metavar="COMMIT")
    parser.add_argument("--packet", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.selftest:
        return run_selftest()
    if args.post_build:
        if not args.packet:

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+src = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+mutants = {
+    "D8.1-status-member": (
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-record-on-other-status": (
+        'if supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+        'if False and supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-status-requires-record": (
+        'if status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+        'if False and status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+    ),
+    "D8.3-5-record-validator": (
+        '            check_superseded_record(path, supersedes[0], checker)',
+        '            pass  # mutant: record validator removed',
+    ),
+    "D8.6-equality": (
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.7-anchor-status": (
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized")',
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+    ),
+    "D8.8-map-status": (
+        'if maps and status != "realized":',
+        'if False and maps and status != "realized":',
+    ),
+    "D8.9-summary-name": (
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                superseded.append(path.name)',
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                pass  # mutant: omit summary name',
+    ),
+}
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    for name, (old, new) in mutants.items():
+        assert old in src, name
+        path = Path(td) / f"{name}.py"
+        path.write_text(src.replace(old, new, 1), encoding="utf-8")
+        p = subprocess.run(["python3", str(path), "--selftest"], text=True, capture_output=True)
+        lines = [line for line in (p.stdout + p.stderr).splitlines() if "selftest" in line]
+        print(f"MUTANT {name}: exit={p.returncode}")
+        for line in lines[-8:]:
+            print("  " + line)
diff --git a//tmp/qa_pending_flip.py b//tmp/qa_pending_flip.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4b4c091c24b5ee50d18862226d50a0cb39196ba6
--- /dev/null
+++ b//tmp/qa_pending_flip.py
@@ -0,0 +1,36 @@
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow")
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td) / "repo"
+    subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--shared", str(SRC), str(root)], check=True)
+    draft = root / "v3/implementation/contracts/ch13-context-block-contract.md"
+    before = draft.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    after = before.replace(
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "ratified"',
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "superseded"',
+        1,
+    )
+    after += '\n```json\n{"superseded": {"date": "2026-08-04", "oracle_branch": "ch13-prose-line", "oracle_tip": "bb313036c7e50ad2625f0669f76a90bf317255e3", "plan": "v3/implementation/ch13-rederivation-plan.md"}}\n```\n'
+    draft.write_text(after, encoding="utf-8")
+    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "add", str(draft)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([
+        "git", "-C", str(root), "-c", "user.email=qa@example.invalid", "-c", "user.name=qa",
+        "commit", "-q", "-m", "simulate pending flip"
+    ], check=True)
+    lint = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"], cwd=root,
+        text=True, capture_output=True
+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")
+    print(lint.stdout, end="")
+    print(lint.stderr, end="")
+    changed = subprocess.run(
+        ["git", "-C", str(root), "show", "--format=", "--numstat", "HEAD"],
+        text=True, capture_output=True, check=True
+    )
+    print("flip_numstat=" + changed.stdout.strip())
+    print("last_lines:")
+    print("\n".join(after.splitlines()[-4:]))
diff --git a//tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py b//tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..062424fae12126382063d12b483bc965b952dcf7
--- /dev/null
+++ b//tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py
@@ -0,0 +1,117 @@
+import importlib.util
+import re
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("packet_lint", SRC)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+ANCHOR = "contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"
+
+def run_channel(name, packet_text, filename="ch9-p1-test.md"):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    target = root / "packets" / filename
+    target.write_text(packet_text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    dedicated = [e for e in errors if "the draft is superseded (terminal)" in e]
+    print(f"CHANNEL {name}: errors={len(errors)} dedicated={len(dedicated)} grandfathered={stats['grandfathered']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+base_no_contract = m.GREEN_PACKET.replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"]', '"refs": ["prose:local"]'
+).replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", "prose:plan §7.2"]',
+    '"refs": ["prose:plan §7.2"]'
+)
+
+run_channel("manifest", m.GREEN_PACKET)
+run_channel("header-only", base_no_contract + f"\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n")
+run_channel("anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("derived-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'derived'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("nested-anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText (anchored: context (see {ANCHOR})).\n")
+run_channel("escaped-rendering-anchor-only", base_no_contract + "\nText (anchored: contract:ch9-test\\-surface#C1).\n")
+run_channel(
+    "anchored-closure+manifest",
+    m.GREEN_PACKET + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+)
+run_channel(
+    "grandfathered-header+closure",
+    f"# old packet\n\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n\nText (anchored: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+    "ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md",
+)
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+for bad_date in ("2026-02-29", "2026-13-40", "0000-00-00"):
+    draft.write_text(text.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', f'"date": "{bad_date}"'), encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"DATE {bad_date}: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+def run_draft_case(name, mutate, commit=False):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    draft.write_text(mutate(text, off), encoding="utf-8")
+    if commit:
+        m._git_ok(root, "add", "-A")
+        m._git_ok(root, "commit", "-q", "-m", name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"CLAIM {name}: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run_draft_case("D8.1-valid", lambda t, off: t)
+run_draft_case("D8.2-record-wrong-type", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-top-level-sibling-key", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"noise": true, "superseded": {', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-date-number", lambda t, off: t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', '"date": 20260803'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-branch-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-tip-number", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"', '"oracle_tip": 7', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-plan-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-empty-branch", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-empty-plan", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.6-row-edit", lambda t, off: t.replace("| C1 | the row |", "| C1 | changed |"), commit=True)
+run_draft_case("D8.8-empty-map", lambda t, off: t + '\n```json\n{"realized_map": {}}\n```\n')
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR, forbid_reopened=True)
+print(f"CLAIM D8.9-summary-and-gate: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td)
+    (root / "packets").mkdir()
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE missing-contracts-dir: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    outside = Path(td)
+    (outside / "contracts").mkdir()
+    (outside / "packets").mkdir()
+    (outside / "contracts" / draft.name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(outside / "packets", outside / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE superseded-outside-git: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()

exec

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+src = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+mutants = {
+    "D8.1-status-member": (
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'DRAFT_STATUSES = ("draft", "ratified", "reopened", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-record-on-other-status": (
+        'if supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+        'if False and supersedes and status != "superseded":',
+    ),
+    "D8.2-status-requires-record": (
+        'if status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+        'if False and status == "superseded":\n        if len(supersedes) != 1:',
+    ),
+    "D8.3-5-record-validator": (
+        '            check_superseded_record(path, supersedes[0], checker)',
+        '            pass  # mutant: record validator removed',
+    ),
+    "D8.6-equality": (
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+        'EQUALITY_BOUND = ("ratified", "realized")',
+    ),
+    "D8.7-anchor-status": (
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized")',
+        'RATIFIED_OR_LATER = ("ratified", "realized", "superseded")',
+    ),
+    "D8.8-map-status": (
+        'if maps and status != "realized":',
+        'if False and maps and status != "realized":',
+    ),
+    "D8.9-summary-name": (
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                superseded.append(path.name)',
+        '            elif info["status"] == "superseded":\n                pass  # mutant: omit summary name',
+    ),
+}
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    for name, (old, new) in mutants.items():
+        assert old in src, name
+        path = Path(td) / f"{name}.py"
+        path.write_text(src.replace(old, new, 1), encoding="utf-8")
+        p = subprocess.run(["python3", str(path), "--selftest"], text=True, capture_output=True)
+        lines = [line for line in (p.stdout + p.stderr).splitlines() if "selftest" in line]
+        print(f"MUTANT {name}: exit={p.returncode}")
+        for line in lines[-8:]:
+            print("  " + line)
diff --git a//tmp/qa_pending_flip.py b//tmp/qa_pending_flip.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..4b4c091c24b5ee50d18862226d50a0cb39196ba6

@@ -0,0 +1,36 @@
+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow")
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td) / "repo"
+    subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--shared", str(SRC), str(root)], check=True)
+    draft = root / "v3/implementation/contracts/ch13-context-block-contract.md"
+    before = draft.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    after = before.replace(
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "ratified"',
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "superseded"',
+        1,
+    )
+    after += '\n```json\n{"superseded": {"date": "2026-08-04", "oracle_branch": "ch13-prose-line", "oracle_tip": "bb313036c7e50ad2625f0669f76a90bf317255e3", "plan": "v3/implementation/ch13-rederivation-plan.md"}}\n```\n'
+    draft.write_text(after, encoding="utf-8")
+    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "add", str(draft)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([
+        "git", "-C", str(root), "-c", "user.email=qa@example.invalid", "-c", "user.name=qa",
+        "commit", "-q", "-m", "simulate pending flip"
+    ], check=True)
+    lint = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"], cwd=root,
+        text=True, capture_output=True
+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")
+    print(lint.stdout, end="")
+    print(lint.stderr, end="")
+    changed = subprocess.run(
+        ["git", "-C", str(root), "show", "--format=", "--numstat", "HEAD"],
+        text=True, capture_output=True, check=True
+    )
+    print("flip_numstat=" + changed.stdout.strip())
+    print("last_lines:")
+    print("\n".join(after.splitlines()[-4:]))
diff --git a//tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py b//tmp/qa_superseded_probe.py

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..062424fae12126382063d12b483bc965b952dcf7

+import importlib.util
+import re
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = "/Users/felho/dev/pairflow/tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"
+spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("packet_lint", SRC)
+m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+spec.loader.exec_module(m)
+
+ANCHOR = "contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"
+
+def run_channel(name, packet_text, filename="ch9-p1-test.md"):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    target = root / "packets" / filename
+    target.write_text(packet_text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    dedicated = [e for e in errors if "the draft is superseded (terminal)" in e]
+    print(f"CHANNEL {name}: errors={len(errors)} dedicated={len(dedicated)} grandfathered={stats['grandfathered']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+base_no_contract = m.GREEN_PACKET.replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C1"]', '"refs": ["prose:local"]'
+).replace(
+    '"refs": ["contract:ch9-test-surface#C2", "prose:plan §7.2"]',
+    '"refs": ["prose:plan §7.2"]'
+)
+
+run_channel("manifest", m.GREEN_PACKET)
+run_channel("header-only", base_no_contract + f"\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n")
+run_channel("anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("derived-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText ({'derived'}: {ANCHOR}).\n")
+run_channel("nested-anchored-closure-only", base_no_contract + f"\nText (anchored: context (see {ANCHOR})).\n")
+run_channel("escaped-rendering-anchor-only", base_no_contract + "\nText (anchored: contract:ch9-test\\-surface#C1).\n")
+run_channel(
+    "anchored-closure+manifest",
+    m.GREEN_PACKET + f"\nText ({'anchored'}: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+)
+run_channel(
+    "grandfathered-header+closure",
+    f"# old packet\n\nDraft anchors: `contract:ch9-test-surface` rows C1\n\nText (anchored: {ANCHOR}).\n",
+    "ch4-p1-domain-and-ports.md",
+)
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+for bad_date in ("2026-02-29", "2026-13-40", "0000-00-00"):
+    draft.write_text(text.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', f'"date": "{bad_date}"'), encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"DATE {bad_date}: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+def run_draft_case(name, mutate, commit=False):
+    fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+    assert fixture is not None
+    tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+    packet.unlink()
+    draft.write_text(mutate(text, off), encoding="utf-8")
+    if commit:
+        m._git_ok(root, "add", "-A")
+        m._git_ok(root, "commit", "-q", "-m", name)
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"CLAIM {name}: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run_draft_case("D8.1-valid", lambda t, off: t)
+run_draft_case("D8.2-record-wrong-type", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-top-level-sibling-key", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"noise": true, "superseded": {', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-date-number", lambda t, off: t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', '"date": 20260803'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-branch-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-tip-number", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"', '"oracle_tip": 7', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-plan-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-empty-branch", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-empty-plan", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.6-row-edit", lambda t, off: t.replace("| C1 | the row |", "| C1 | changed |"), commit=True)
+run_draft_case("D8.8-empty-map", lambda t, off: t + '\n```json\n{"realized_map": {}}\n```\n')
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR, forbid_reopened=True)
+print(f"CLAIM D8.9-summary-and-gate: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td)
+    (root / "packets").mkdir()
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE missing-contracts-dir: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    outside = Path(td)
+    (outside / "contracts").mkdir()
+    (outside / "packets").mkdir()
+    (outside / "contracts" / draft.name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
+    errors, _ = m.lint(outside / "packets", outside / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE superseded-outside-git: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+tmp.cleanup()

+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"CLAIM {name}: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+    tmp.cleanup()
+
+run_draft_case("D8.1-valid", lambda t, off: t)
+run_draft_case("D8.2-record-wrong-type", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'\{"superseded": \{[^\n]+\}\}', '{"superseded": []}', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-top-level-sibling-key", lambda t, off: t.replace('{"superseded": {', '{"noise": true, "superseded": {', 1))
+run_draft_case("D8.3-date-number", lambda t, off: t.replace('"date": "2026-08-03"', '"date": 20260803'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-branch-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-tip-number", lambda t, off: re.sub(r'"oracle_tip": "[0-9a-f]{40}"', '"oracle_tip": 7', t))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-plan-list", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": []'))
+run_draft_case("D8.4-empty-branch", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"oracle_branch": "{m.ORACLE_BRANCH}"', '"oracle_branch": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.5-empty-plan", lambda t, off: t.replace(f'"plan": "{m.ORACLE_PLAN}"', '"plan": ""'))
+run_draft_case("D8.6-row-edit", lambda t, off: t.replace("| C1 | the row |", "| C1 | changed |"), commit=True)
+run_draft_case("D8.8-empty-map", lambda t, off: t + '\n```json\n{"realized_map": {}}\n```\n')
+
+fixture = m.build_superseded_fixture()
+assert fixture is not None
+tmp, root, draft, packet, text, off = fixture
+packet.unlink()
+errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR, forbid_reopened=True)
+print(f"CLAIM D8.9-summary-and-gate: errors={len(errors)} superseded={stats['superseded']}")
+tmp.cleanup()
+

+    root = Path(td)
+    (root / "packets").mkdir()
+    errors, stats = m.lint(root / "packets", root / "contracts", m.ADR_DIR)
+    print(f"EDGE missing-contracts-dir: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:
+        print(f"  {e}")
+

+    (outside / "packets").mkdir()

+    print(f"EDGE superseded-outside-git: errors={len(errors)}")
+    for e in errors:

+import subprocess
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+SRC = Path("/Users/felho/dev/pairflow")
+with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
+    root = Path(td) / "repo"
+    subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--shared", str(SRC), str(root)], check=True)
+    draft = root / "v3/implementation/contracts/ch13-context-block-contract.md"
+    before = draft.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    after = before.replace(
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "ratified"',
+        '"surface": "context-block", "status": "superseded"',
+        1,
+    )
+    after += '\n```json\n{"superseded": {"date": "2026-08-04", "oracle_branch": "ch13-prose-line", "oracle_tip": "bb313036c7e50ad2625f0669f76a90bf317255e3", "plan": "v3/implementation/ch13-rederivation-plan.md"}}\n```\n'
+    draft.write_text(after, encoding="utf-8")
+    subprocess.run(["git", "-C", str(root), "add", str(draft)], check=True)
+    subprocess.run([
+        "git", "-C", str(root), "-c", "user.email=qa@example.invalid", "-c", "user.name=qa",
+        "commit", "-q", "-m", "simulate pending flip"
+    ], check=True)
+    lint = subprocess.run(
+        ["python3", "tools/v3-plan/check_packet.py"], cwd=root,
+        text=True, capture_output=True
+    )
+    print(f"lint_exit={lint.returncode}")
+    print(lint.stdout, end="")
+    print(lint.stderr, end="")
+    changed = subprocess.run(
+        ["git", "-C", str(root), "show", "--format=", "--numstat", "HEAD"],
+        text=True, capture_output=True, check=True
+    )
+    print("flip_numstat=" + changed.stdout.strip())
+    print("last_lines:")
+    print("\n".join(after.splitlines()[-4:]))

+import importlib.util
